# Daily video prompt pack — 22/11/2026 · Rat day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំជូត)

Lucky signs: Rat, Dragon, Monkey · Careful: Horse
Day element: Yang Metal ✨ (Geng-zi day; ធាតុលោហៈ) · every action scene today carries Metal magic: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 22/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Rat's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង
ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ និងធ្វើអ្វីៗដោយទំនុកចិត្ត។
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។
ការងារ៖ អតិថិជន និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។
លុយកាក់៖ ល្អសម្រាប់កិច្ចព្រមព្រៀងយុត្តិធម៌ និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។ សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។
ដំបូន្មាន៖ សូមទទួលយកឱកាសល្អមួយនៅថ្ងៃនេះ ហើយអរគុណអ្នកដែលបានជួយអ្នក។

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Rat, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Rat as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Rat's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Rat and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Rat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូតមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Rat, people may describe you as quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ឆ្លាតវៃ ប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងចូលចិត្តសេពគប់។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Rat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Sunday, 22 November 2026, is a Rat day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rat. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Rat are quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Rats are natural problem-solvers who notice opportunities others miss.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Rat? Your luck is strong today. Stay bright, clever and lively. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear something gold, or carry a small gold item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southwest. When you start something new, face the southwest, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 3, 20, and 323. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 3, 20, 323!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Rat's lucky days of the week are Monday and Tuesday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Rat can be restless or overly cautious with money and trust. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 3, 20, and 323.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខបី លេខម្ភៃ និងលេខបីរយម្ភៃបី។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – Khmer (2026-11-22-180s.srt)

```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,392

4

ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ទី 22 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

00:00:12,748 --> 00:00:14,835

6

7
00:00:18,092 --> 00:00:19,950

8

9
00:00:21,806 --> 00:00:23,563
ហើយជាសត្វទីមួយនៃរាសីទាំង 12។

10

11
00:00:25,161 --> 00:00:27,305

12
00:00:27,355 --> 00:00:29,950

13

14
00:00:33,370 --> 00:00:36,472

15

16

18

សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25

ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:11,450

28
00:01:11,500 --> 00:01:14,750
ល្អសម្រាប់កិច្ចព្រមព្រៀងយុត្តិធម៌

29
00:01:14,800 --> 00:01:17,450
និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។

30
00:01:17,500 --> 00:01:19,950
សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។

31
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,923
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌មាស។

32
00:01:22,973 --> 00:01:26,076

33

ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34

ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

35

36
00:01:34,956 --> 00:01:37,472
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

37
00:01:37,522 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

38

39

40

ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

41
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677

42
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314

43
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950

44
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,132

45
00:02:03,182 --> 00:02:05,495
គឺថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃអង្គារ។

46
00:02:05,545 --> 00:02:07,223
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

47
00:02:07,273 --> 00:02:09,950
សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

48
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,045
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត

49
00:02:12,095 --> 00:02:13,396
ជួនកាលមិនស្ងប់ស្ងាត់

50

ឬប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេកចំពោះប្រាក់កាស

51
00:02:15,946 --> 00:02:16,842
និងទំនុកចិត្ត។

52
00:02:16,892 --> 00:02:18,464
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

53
00:02:18,514 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

54
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

55
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

56
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

57
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

58
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

59
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

60
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

61
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

62
00:02:41,654 --> 00:02:42,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

63
00:02:42,992 --> 00:02:45,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

64
00:02:45,827 --> 00:02:47,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

65
00:02:47,323 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 3 លេខ 20 និងលេខ 323។

66
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

67
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

68
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

69
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

70
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-22-180s.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,543
Born in the Year of the Rat?

2
00:00:02,593 --> 00:00:04,672
Here's your lucky color

3
00:00:04,722 --> 00:00:07,635
best time, and numbers for today

4
00:00:07,685 --> 00:00:09,950
plus one gentle reminder.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,372
Today, Sunday, 22 November 2026

6
00:00:12,422 --> 00:00:13,388
is a Rat day.

7
00:00:13,438 --> 00:00:16,122
It is a Great Day for everyone born

8
00:00:16,172 --> 00:00:17,919
in the Year of the Rat.

9
00:00:17,969 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,328
Clever and quick, the first animal

11
00:00:22,378 --> 00:00:24,915
of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit

12
00:00:24,965 --> 00:00:27,433
resourcefulness, and new beginnings.

13
00:00:27,483 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,535
People born in the Year of the Rat are

15
00:00:32,585 --> 00:00:35,256
quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable.

16
00:00:35,306 --> 00:00:37,705
Rats are natural problem-solvers who

17
00:00:37,755 --> 00:00:39,950
notice opportunities others miss.

18
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,689
Born in the Year of the Rat?

19
00:00:41,739 --> 00:00:43,304
Your luck is strong today.

20
00:00:43,354 --> 00:00:45,230
Stay bright, clever and lively.

21
00:00:45,280 --> 00:00:47,341
Say yes to good chances, and share

22
00:00:47,391 --> 00:00:49,950
your happiness with the people around you.

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,394
Love and family today:

24
00:00:52,444 --> 00:00:56,506
A good day to reach out, share a meal

25
00:00:56,556 --> 00:00:59,950
and show your affection openly.

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,969
Work today: Customers

27
00:01:02,019 --> 00:01:05,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

28
00:01:05,288 --> 00:01:08,315
A good day to present your ideas

29
00:01:08,365 --> 00:01:09,950
and close a deal.

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,805
Money today: Good for fair deals

31
00:01:13,855 --> 00:01:17,179
and collecting what is owed.

32
00:01:17,229 --> 00:01:19,950
Keep your records neat.

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,718
Your lucky color today is gold.

34
00:01:22,768 --> 00:01:26,468
Wear something gold, or carry a small gold

35
00:01:26,518 --> 00:01:29,950
item with you, to keep good luck close.

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,659
Your best time today

37
00:01:31,709 --> 00:01:34,394
is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

38
00:01:34,444 --> 00:01:37,044
It is a good time for important

39
00:01:37,094 --> 00:01:39,950
plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

40
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:41,431
Your lucky direction

41
00:01:41,481 --> 00:01:43,135
today is the southwest.

42
00:01:43,185 --> 00:01:45,209
When you start something new

43
00:01:45,259 --> 00:01:47,876
face the southwest, and walk forward

44
00:01:47,926 --> 00:01:49,950
with a calm and happy heart.

45
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,350
For lucky numbers: 3, 20, and 323.

46
00:01:53,400 --> 00:01:56,450
Comment your lucky number below

47
00:01:56,500 --> 00:01:59,950
and let's say it again: 3, 20, 323!

48
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,118
The Rat's lucky days of the week

49
00:02:03,168 --> 00:02:05,396
are Monday and Tuesday.

50
00:02:05,446 --> 00:02:08,069
Keep them in mind for plans

51
00:02:08,119 --> 00:02:09,950
that matter to you.

52
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,911
A gentle reminder: people born

53
00:02:11,961 --> 00:02:14,394
in the Year of the Rat can be restless

54
00:02:14,444 --> 00:02:17,009
or overly cautious with money and trust.

55
00:02:17,059 --> 00:02:18,316
Today, stay balanced

56
00:02:18,366 --> 00:02:19,950
```

## Voice-over – long YouTube video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Rat and the Silent Temple Bell" (កណ្ដុរមាស និងកណ្ដឹងប្រាសាទដែលស្ងាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden rat and Kong the little frog must bring the bell's voice back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Kong once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Rat picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Kong and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Kong the little frog (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Kong, a tiny, lifelike magical green-and-gold frog with bright, expressive eyes, a wide happy smile and a little lotus-leaf hat, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

An original animated bedtime story starring the Rat, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the rat is the saved Flow character @Rat. Her face is full of genuine excitement, as if sharing wonderful news with a close friend: her eyes light up, her eyebrows lift and a bright, natural smile spreads across her face as she leans slightly toward the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical.

Chanda is heard as a voice-over only: she is not on screen, and no one on screen speaks or moves their mouth. She says in Khmer: "នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!" ## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden rat bring the bell's voice back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the bell had lost its voice. When it was struck, there was only silence. Without its song, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden rat. Today was a Rat day, and the rat felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Rat," he said, "only you can bring the bell's voice back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the golden bell, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the rat, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Rat are quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Rats are natural problem-solvers who notice opportunities others miss.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was gold, the rat's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The rat tied a gold ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the rat's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The rat stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Rat can be restless or overly cautious with money and trust.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the rat climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Rat today, that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the rat rang the golden bells 3 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The rat climbed 20 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a small golden crystal, humming a soft song. It was the heart of the bell!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the crystal, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Monday and Tuesday shone brightest, the Rat's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the crystal carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The rat placed the crystal inside the bell. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, dong! A deep, sweet sound rang across the land!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The bell sang again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The rat felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Rat, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 3, 20, and 323.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ដើម្បីដាស់កណ្ដឹងមាស វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រីស្តាល់យ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
កណ្ដុរដាក់គ្រីស្តាល់ចូលក្នុងកណ្ដឹង។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក ដុង! សំឡេងដ៏ពីរោះ បន្លឺឡើងពាសពេញទឹកដី!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ កណ្ដុរមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខបី លេខម្ភៃ និងលេខបីរយម្ភៃបី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

```
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

91
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

92
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

93
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,132
កណ្ដុរដាក់គ្រីស្តាល់ចូលក្នុងកណ្ដឹង។

94
00:03:53,182 --> 00:03:55,677
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

95
00:03:55,727 --> 00:03:56,950
បន្ទាប់មក ដុង!

96
00:03:57,000 --> 00:03:59,950
សំឡេងដ៏ពីរោះ បន្លឺឡើងពាសពេញទឹកដី!

97
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,677
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។

98
00:04:01,727 --> 00:04:02,950
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

99
00:04:03,000 --> 00:04:06,495
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

100
00:04:06,545 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

101
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,070
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

102
00:04:13,120 --> 00:04:14,910
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

103
00:04:14,960 --> 00:04:17,870
កណ្ដុរមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

104
00:04:17,920 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

105
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

106
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

107
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

108
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

109
00:04:31,654 --> 00:04:32,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

110
00:04:32,992 --> 00:04:35,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

111
00:04:35,827 --> 00:04:37,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

112
00:04:37,323 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 3 លេខ 20 និងលេខ 323។

113
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

114
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

115
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

116
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

117
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

118
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

119
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

120
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-22-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,713
Can one little golden rat bring the bell's

2
00:00:02,763 --> 00:00:05,213

3
00:00:05,263 --> 00:00:07,976
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:08,026 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,181
But this morning

6
00:00:11,231 --> 00:00:13,335

7
00:00:13,385 --> 00:00:14,719
When it was struck

8
00:00:14,769 --> 00:00:16,488

9

10
00:00:17,769 --> 00:00:19,950

11

In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

12
00:00:23,015 --> 00:00:25,979

13
00:00:26,029 --> 00:00:27,376
Today was a Rat day

14
00:00:27,426 --> 00:00:29,950

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,412
A little frog named

16
00:00:31,462 --> 00:00:33,181

17
00:00:33,231 --> 00:00:34,796
"Little Rat," he said

18
00:00:34,846 --> 00:00:38,027

19
00:00:38,077 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

20
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,049

00:00:42,099 --> 00:00:44,333

22
00:00:44,383 --> 00:00:46,555

23
00:00:46,605 --> 00:00:47,296
Kong smiled.

24
00:00:47,346 --> 00:00:48,530
That was exactly why

25
00:00:48,580 --> 00:00:49,950

26
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,819
To wake the golden bell, the little

27
00:00:52,869 --> 00:00:55,606

28
00:00:55,656 --> 00:00:58,147

29
00:00:58,197 --> 00:00:59,950

30
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,842

31
00:01:02,892 --> 00:01:06,577

32
00:01:06,627 --> 00:01:09,950

33
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,535

34
00:01:12,585 --> 00:01:15,256
quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable.

35
00:01:15,306 --> 00:01:17,705
Rats are natural problem-solvers who

36
00:01:17,755 --> 00:01:19,950

37
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,240
The first treasure was waiting

38
00:01:22,290 --> 00:01:24,912

39

40
00:01:26,870 --> 00:01:29,950
It was gold, the rat's lucky color today.

41
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,662
The rat tied a gold

42
00:01:31,712 --> 00:01:33,734

43
00:01:33,784 --> 00:01:36,797

44
00:01:36,847 --> 00:01:39,950

45

46
00:01:42,520 --> 00:01:43,771

47

48
00:01:45,691 --> 00:01:47,836

49

50

51
00:01:52,143 --> 00:01:53,576

52
00:01:53,626 --> 00:01:54,455

53
00:01:54,505 --> 00:01:55,664

54

55

56

57

59
00:02:06,827 --> 00:02:09,950

60

62

63

64

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413

68

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950

70

"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,587 --> 00:02:43,918

72

73

74

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,529

76

77

78

88

89

90

93

94

95

96
00:03:37,500 --> 00:03:39,950

97

98

99
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,013
The rat placed the crystal

102

103

104

107

108
00:04:03,770 --> 00:04:06,425

109
00:04:06,475 --> 00:04:07,737
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Horse (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Horse, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Horse clashes with the Rat day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Horse as ingredients (the Horse is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the horse hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little golden firefly keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Horse? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមី រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Horse, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.

3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Horse, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមី នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Horse? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Horse.

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. Continuing from the previous scene, on the same terrace: a two-shot of Chanda and @Horse (the Horse guardian with their horse), the same horse as in every scene, together and both clearly in frame, the horse sitting calm and smiling right beside her. As she speaks, Chanda gently holds out her hands to the little golden firefly from the earlier scenes; it rises up between them and bursts softly into a shower of golden light and glowing lotus petals that drift down around them, wrapping the horse in a warm, gentle golden glow. The horse looks up at the light, relaxes and smiles, then waves goodbye together with Chanda, without speaking, its mouth closed. Lanterns float up over the pond behind them. No other animals. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the horse is the saved Flow character @Horse. Use the saved Flow character @Chanda exactly as she is: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene. Her face shows quiet compassion: she holds one hand open toward the viewer as if offering comfort, and smiles reassuringly. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Sky: a single bright shooting star crosses the sky, leaving a long trail of golden sparks that drift down like snow; it stays in the background and never covers a face.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Horse? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 22 November, is a Rat day, opposite the Horse in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be impatient or struggle to commit long-term. Pause before you react. Small steps still count.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Rat) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Dragon and Monkey), the sign to take it easy (Horse) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Rat
2:40 Good Day: Dragon
3:30 Good Day: Monkey
4:20 Take it easy: Horse
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
★ 3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Horse? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមីមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Rat has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំជូតមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Horse? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Rat day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Rat, Dragon and Monkey are lucky today, and the Horse should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Sunday, 22 November: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Rat! Let's see what this lucky day brings you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Clever and quick, the first animal of the zodiac, the Rat symbolizes wit, resourcefulness, and new beginnings.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Rat are often described as quick-witted, resourceful, and sociable. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
This is your day. Small good choices add up to a lucky day.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Today's lucky color is gold. A gold scarf, bag or bracelet keeps it close. It brings calm and confidence to your day.

The Rat's lucky days of the week are Monday and Tuesday. Keep them for plans that matter. Keep a calm heart.

ថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ថ្ងៃទីម្ភៃពីរ ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត! តោះ មកមើលថា ថ្ងៃសំណាងនេះ នាំអ្វីមកឲ្យអ្នក។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ឆ្នាំជូត ឆ្លាតវៃ និងរហ័សរហួន ហើយជាសត្វទីមួយនៃរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ។ ឆ្នាំជូតតំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា ភាពប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា ឆ្លាតវៃ ប៉ិនប្រសប់ និងចូលចិត្តសេពគប់។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ល្អសម្រាប់កិច្ចព្រមព្រៀងយុត្តិធម៌ និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។ សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌មាស។ កន្សែង កាបូប ឬខ្សែដៃពណ៌មាស នាំសំណាងមកជិតអ្នក។ វានាំភាពស្ងប់ និងទំនុកចិត្ត មកថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសនិរតី។ សូមរៀបចំផែនការតាមនោះ។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
លេខសំណាង៖ បី ម្ភៃ និងបីរយម្ភៃបី។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំជូត គឺថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃអង្គារ។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ជួនកាលមិនស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ឬប្រុងប្រយ័ត្នខ្លាំងពេកចំពោះប្រាក់កាស និងទំនុកចិត្ត។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំរោង ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំជូត។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ប្រផេះ ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងបី រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ល្ងាច។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំពីរ ដប់មួយ និងមួយរយហុកសិបបី។ សូមញញឹម ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំវក ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំជូត។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ស ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំបួន កៅសិបបួន និងបួនរយកៅសិបប្រាំបី។ សូមញញឹម ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទីម្ភៃពីរ ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំជូត ទល់មុខឆ្នាំមមី ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រញាប់ប្រញាល់ ឬពិបាកក្នុងការសន្យារយៈពេលវែង។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 33 (5:20–5:30)
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ល្ងាច។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 34 (5:30–5:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

Scene 35 (5:40–5:50)
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន យប់។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 36 (5:50–6:00)
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 37 (6:00–6:10)
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ យប់។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 38 (6:10–6:20)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 39 (6:20–6:30)
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខ្មៅ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ល្ងាច។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 40 (6:30–6:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 41 (6:40–6:50)
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ យប់។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 42 (6:50–7:00)

```
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត។

75
00:03:25,054 --> 00:03:28,122
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

76
00:03:28,172 --> 00:03:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

77
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,962
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

78
00:03:33,012 --> 00:03:38,384
ឆ្នាំវក ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំជូត។

79
00:03:38,434 --> 00:03:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

80
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,258
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

81
00:03:42,308 --> 00:03:43,967
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

82
00:03:44,017 --> 00:03:44,993
ស្នេហា៖

83
00:03:45,043 --> 00:03:48,326
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

84
00:03:48,376 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

85
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,817
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ស

86
00:03:51,867 --> 00:03:57,017
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

87
00:03:57,067 --> 00:03:59,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

88
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,242
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

89
00:04:02,292 --> 00:04:04,638
លេខសំណាង៖ 9 94 និង 498។

90
00:04:04,688 --> 00:04:05,888
សូមញញឹម

91
00:04:05,938 --> 00:04:09,950
ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

92
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,450
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ

93
00:04:12,500 --> 00:04:15,177
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត។

94
00:04:15,227 --> 00:04:18,473
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

95
00:04:18,523 --> 00:04:19,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

96
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,824
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 22 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំជូត

97
00:04:23,874 --> 00:04:26,617
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំមមី ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

98
00:04:26,667 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

99
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

100
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

101
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

102
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

103
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,283
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រញាប់ប្រញាល់

104
00:04:43,333 --> 00:04:45,700
ឬពិបាកក្នុងការសន្យារយៈពេលវែង។

105
00:04:45,750 --> 00:04:47,950
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

106
00:04:48,000 --> 00:04:49,950
ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

107
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

108
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

109
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

110
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

111
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

112
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

113
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

114
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

115
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

116
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

117
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,534
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

118
00:05:22,584 --> 00:05:24,557
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

119
00:05:24,607 --> 00:05:28,040
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ល្ងាច។

120
00:05:28,090 --> 00:05:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

121
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:34,207
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

122
00:05:34,257 --> 00:05:36,188
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។

123
00:05:36,238 --> 00:05:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

124
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:42,392
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

125
00:05:42,442 --> 00:05:44,369
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

126
00:05:44,419 --> 00:05:47,741
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 យប់។

127
00:05:47,791 --> 00:05:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

128
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:50,910
ស្នេហា៖

129
00:05:50,960 --> 00:05:54,030
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

130
00:05:54,080 --> 00:05:56,590
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

131
00:05:56,640 --> 00:05:58,190
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

132
00:05:58,240 --> 00:05:59,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

133
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,364
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

134
00:06:02,414 --> 00:06:04,548
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

135
00:06:04,598 --> 00:06:07,996
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 យប់។

136
00:06:08,046 --> 00:06:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

137
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:14,338
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

138
00:06:14,388 --> 00:06:16,379
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

139
00:06:16,429 --> 00:06:19,950
ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

140
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,559
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

141
00:06:22,609 --> 00:06:24,515
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខ្មៅ។

142
00:06:24,565 --> 00:06:27,885
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ល្ងាច។

143
00:06:27,935 --> 00:06:29,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

144
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:33,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

145
00:06:33,413 --> 00:06:37,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

146
00:06:37,063 --> 00:06:38,601
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។

147
00:06:38,651 --> 00:06:39,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

148
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,364
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

149
00:06:42,414 --> 00:06:44,548
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

150
00:06:44,598 --> 00:06:47,996
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 យប់។

151
00:06:48,046 --> 00:06:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

152
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:53,969
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

153
00:06:54,019 --> 00:06:55,838
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

154
00:06:55,888 --> 00:06:59,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ ជាថ្ងៃនៃភាពច្បាស់លាស់។

155
00:07:00,000 --> 00:07:02,258
ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

156
00:07:02,308 --> 00:07:04,346
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

157
00:07:04,396 --> 00:07:07,862
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

158
00:07:07,912 --> 00:07:09,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

159
00:07:10,000 --> 00:07:13,418
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

160
00:07:13,468 --> 00:07:17,127
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

161
00:07:17,177 --> 00:07:18,902
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

162
00:07:18,952 --> 00:07:19,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

163
00:07:20,000 --> 00:07:22,483
ឆ្នាំច៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

164
00:07:22,533 --> 00:07:25,017
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

165
00:07:25,067 --> 00:07:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

166
00:07:30,000 --> 00:07:30,967
ស្នេហា៖

167
00:07:31,017 --> 00:07:34,272
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

168
00:07:34,322 --> 00:07:36,984
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

169
00:07:37,034 --> 00:07:38,848
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

170
00:07:38,898 --> 00:07:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

171
00:07:40,000 --> 00:07:42,364
ឆ្នាំកុរ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

172
00:07:42,414 --> 00:07:44,318
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

173
00:07:44,368 --> 00:07:47,766
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

174
00:07:47,816 --> 00:07:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

175
00:07:50,000 --> 00:07:53,390
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

176
00:07:53,440 --> 00:07:57,070
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

177
00:07:57,120 --> 00:07:58,590
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

178
00:07:58,640 --> 00:07:59,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

179
00:08:00,000 --> 00:08:03,027
សរុបមកវិញ៖ ឆ្នាំជូតថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង

180
00:08:03,077 --> 00:08:05,527
ឆ្នាំរោង និងឆ្នាំវកថ្ងៃល្អ

181
00:08:05,577 --> 00:08:08,315
ហើយឆ្នាំមមី គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។

182
00:08:08,365 --> 00:08:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

183
00:08:10,000 --> 00:08:12,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

184
00:08:12,564 --> 00:08:16,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។
```

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

## Scene 1 – Rat day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Her face is full of genuine excitement, as if sharing wonderful news with a close friend: her eyes light up, her eyebrows lift and a bright, natural smile spreads across her face as she leans slightly toward the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it vanishes in a puff of golden smoke and reappears on its guardian's hand, who spins through the air throwing glowing golden stars that burst into sparkling fireworks over the pond, as today's Metal magic swirls around them: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and mist swirls away in glowing rings. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat), @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) and @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, long ribbons of golden light unfurl from the temple towers and wave above them. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Horse, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Horse (the Horse guardian with their horse) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it curls up calmly as glowing butterflies circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The horse is the saved Flow character @Horse.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Long golden ribbons of light unfurl from the temple towers and wave across the starry sky, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
